import socket, time, threading
from pwnlib.tubes.listen import listen
from pwnlib.tubes.remote import remote

l = listen(0); r = remote('localhost', l.lport); srv = l.wait_for_connection()
m1 = r.mux(max_channels=5)
m2 = srv.mux(max_channels=5)

# auto ids
a = m1.open_channel(timeout=1)
b = m1.open_channel(timeout=1)
c = m1.open_channel(timeout=1)
print("auto ids", a.channel_id, b.channel_id, c.channel_id)

# non-int
for bad in [1.0, "1", True, None if False else 2.0]:
    try:
        m1.open_channel(bad, timeout=1)
        print("ERROR no TypeError", bad)
    except TypeError:
        print("TypeError ok", bad)
    except Exception as e:
        print("wrong exc", bad, type(e).__name__)

# out of range / duplicate / capacity
try:
    m1.open_channel(0, timeout=1)
    print("ERROR id 0")
except ValueError:
    print("ValueError id0 ok")
try:
    m1.open_channel(65536, timeout=1)
    print("ERROR id 65536")
except ValueError:
    print("ValueError id65536 ok")
try:
    m1.open_channel(a.channel_id, timeout=1)
    print("ERROR dup")
except ValueError:
    print("ValueError dup ok")
# capacity: max_channels=5, already 3 open, id 5 should work? capacity 5. open id 6 (> max) ValueError
try:
    m1.open_channel(6, timeout=1)
    print("ERROR id 6 > max")
except ValueError:
    print("ValueError id6 ok")
# open id 4 and 5 -> 5 total
d = m1.open_channel(4, timeout=1)
e = m1.open_channel(5, timeout=1)
try:
    m1.open_channel(timeout=1)
    print("ERROR capacity auto")
except ValueError:
    print("ValueError capacity auto ok")

# accept on m2 should have 5 pending channels
for i in range(5):
    ch = m2.accept_channel(timeout=1)
    print("accepted", i, ch.channel_id if ch else None)
extra = m2.accept_channel(timeout=0.2)
print("accept timeout returns", extra)

# open timeout when no remote accepts? actually remote auto-acks, so no timeout.
# Create a mux whose peer isn't a mux -> open times out
l2 = listen(0); r2 = remote('localhost', l2.lport); srv2 = l2.wait_for_connection()
m3 = r2.mux()
t0 = time.time()
try:
    m3.open_channel(1, timeout=0.2)
    print("ERROR no timeout")
except TimeoutError:
    print("TimeoutError open ok", round(time.time()-t0,2))
except Exception as e:
    print("wrong exc open", type(e).__name__, e)
m3.close(); r2.close(); l2.close()

# close unblocks accept
l3 = listen(0); r3 = remote('localhost', l3.lport); srv3 = l3.wait_for_connection()
m4 = r3.mux(); m5 = srv3.mux()
result = {}
def acc():
    try:
        m5.accept_channel(timeout=10)
        result['r'] = 'no exc'
    except EOFError:
        result['r'] = 'EOFError'
    except Exception as e:
        result['r'] = type(e).__name__
th = threading.Thread(target=acc); th.start()
time.sleep(0.2)
m4.close()
th.join(2)
print("close unblocks accept:", result.get('r'))

m1.close(); m2.close()
r.close(); l.close()
print("IDS OK")

import socket, time, threading
from pwnlib.tubes.listen import listen
from pwnlib.tubes.remote import remote

l3 = listen(0); r3 = remote('localhost', l3.lport); srv3 = l3.wait_for_connection()
m4 = r3.mux(); m5 = srv3.mux()
print("m5 closed initial", m5.closed)
def acc():
    print("accept thread entering")
    try:
        x = m5.accept_channel(timeout=10)
        print("accept returned", x)
    except EOFError:
        print("accept EOFError")
    except Exception as e:
        print("accept exc", type(e).__name__, e)
th = threading.Thread(target=acc); th.start()
time.sleep(0.2)
print("closing m4")
m4.close()
for i in range(10):
    time.sleep(0.2)
    print("m5.closed", m5.closed, "alive", th.is_alive())
    if not th.is_alive():
        break
th.join(1)
print("done", m5.closed)

import socket, time, threading
ls = socket.socket(); ls.bind(('127.0.0.1',0)); ls.listen(1)

def recv_thread():
    try:
        d = c.recv(100)

    except Exception as e:

cd /app && python - <<'PYEOF'
path = 'pwnlib/tubes/mux.py'
s = open(path).read()

# Add _MSG_MUX_CLOSE
s = s.replace("""_MSG_FIN    = 8   # Half-close: local send direction is finished
""", """_MSG_FIN    = 8   # Half-close: local send direction is finished
_MSG_MUX_CLOSE = 9  # The whole multiplexer is being closed
""")

# Update close()
old_close = '''    def close(self):
        """Close the multiplexer and all channels.

        Signals EOF to every channel, closes the underlying tube, and is
        idempotent.  Threads blocked in :meth:`accept_channel` are woken up
        with :class:`EOFError`.
        """
        with self._closed_cond:
            if self._closed:
                return
            self._closed = True
            self._closed_cond.notify_all()

        with self._channels_lock:
            channels = list(self.channels.values())
            self.channels.clear()

        for channel in channels:
            channel._mux_closed()

        with self._accept_cond:
            self._accept_cond.notify_all()

        try:
            self._underlying.close()
        except Exception:
            pass
'''
new_close = '''    def close(self):
        """Close the multiplexer and all channels.

        Signals EOF to every channel, closes the underlying tube, and is
        idempotent.  Threads blocked in :meth:`accept_channel` are woken up
        with :class:`EOFError`.
        """
        with self._closed_cond:
            if self._closed:
                return

        # Tell the peer that the entire multiplexer is closing, so that it
        # can detect the closure even if the underlying tube's EOF is delayed.
        try:
            self._send_frame(0, _MSG_MUX_CLOSE)
        except Exception:
            pass

        with self._closed_cond:
            if self._closed:
                return
            self._closed = True
            self._closed_cond.notify_all()

        with self._channels_lock:
            channels = list(self.channels.values())
            self.channels.clear()

        for channel in channels:
            channel._mux_closed()

        with self._accept_cond:
            self._accept_cond.notify_all()

        # Wake our own reader thread and signal EOF to the peer.  A blocked
        # recv() is woken by shutting down the socket, whereas merely calling
        # close() may leave the reader thread stuck in recv().
        try:
            self._underlying.shutdown("send")
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            self._underlying.shutdown("recv")
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            self._underlying.close()
        except Exception:
            pass
'''
assert old_close in s
s = s.replace(old_close, new_close, 1)

# Add dispatch case
old_dispatch = '''        elif msgtype == _MSG_RESUME:
            self._handle_resume(channel_id)
'''
new_dispatch = '''        elif msgtype == _MSG_RESUME:
            self._handle_resume(channel_id)
        elif msgtype == _MSG_MUX_CLOSE:
            self._handle_underlying_eof()
'''
assert old_dispatch in s
s = s.replace(old_dispatch, new_dispatch, 1)

open(path, 'w').write(s)
print("patched")

patched
ok

import socket, time, threading
from pwnlib.tubes.listen import listen
from pwnlib.tubes.remote import remote

l = listen(0); r = remote('localhost', l.lport); srv = l.wait_for_connection()
m1 = r.mux(high_water_mark=100, low_water_mark=10)
m2 = srv.mux(high_water_mark=100, low_water_mark=10)
c1 = m1.open_channel(1, timeout=1)
c2 = m2.accept_channel(timeout=1)
c1.settimeout(0.3)
c2.settimeout(0.3)

# Send a chunk that puts c2 over high watermark
c1.send(b'A' * 200)
# wait for pause to propagate
t0=time.time()
while not c2._flow_paused and time.time()-t0 < 1:
    time.sleep(0.01)
print("c2 flow_paused", c2._flow_paused, "recv_size", c2._recv_size)

# Now c1 send should block then TimeoutError
t0=time.time()
try:
    c1.send(b'B'*10)
    print("ERROR: expected TimeoutError")
except TimeoutError:
    print("TimeoutError ok after", round(time.time()-t0, 3))
except Exception as e:
    print("wrong exc", type(e).__name__, e)

# Now read on c2, should trigger resume
data = c2.recv()
print("received", len(data), "bytes; flow_paused now", c2._flow_paused, "recv_size", c2._recv_size)
t0=time.time()
while c2._flow_paused and time.time()-t0 < 1:
    time.sleep(0.01)
print("after drain flow_paused", c2._flow_paused)

# c1 should now send successfully
c1.send(b'C'*10)
print("c2 recv2:", c2.recv())
print("stats c1", c1.stats, "c2", c2.stats)

# Per-channel independence: open a second channel, pause it, verify other can still send
a1 = m1.open_channel(2, timeout=1); a2 = m2.accept_channel(timeout=1)
a1.settimeout(0.2); a2.settimeout(0.2)
b1 = m1.open_channel(3, timeout=1); b2 = m2.accept_channel(timeout=1)
b1.settimeout(0.2); b2.settimeout(0.2)
a1.send(b'X'*200)
time.sleep(0.2)
print("a2 flow_paused", a2._flow_paused)
# b1 should still send fine even though a is paused
b1.send(b'Y'*10)
print("b2 got", b2.recv())
# a1 should time out
try:
    a1.send(b'Z'*10)
    print("ERROR a1 expected timeout")
except TimeoutError:
    print("a1 independent TimeoutError ok")

m1.close(); m2.close()
print("FLOW OK")

import socket, time, threading
from pwnlib.tubes.listen import listen
from pwnlib.tubes.remote import remote

l = listen(0); r = remote('localhost', l.lport); srv = l.wait_for_connection()
m1 = r.mux(); m2 = srv.mux()
c1 = m1.open_channel(1, timeout=1); c2 = m2.accept_channel(timeout=1)

print("initial connected", c1.connected(), c1.connected('send'), c1.connected('recv'))
c1.shutdown('send')
print("after shutdown send c1:", c1.connected('send'), c1.connected('recv'), c1.connected())
# c1 send should raise EOFError
try:
    c1.send(b'x')
    print("ERROR c1 send after shutdown")
except EOFError:
    print("c1 send EOF ok")
# c1 can still recv
c2.send(b'hello')
print("c1 recv after shutdown:", c1.recv())

# remote c2 recv should EOF after c1 shutdown('send')
try:
    c2.recv(timeout=0.5)
    print("ERROR c2 recv expected EOF")
except EOFError:
    print("c2 recv EOF ok")
# c2 can still send
c2.send(b'world')
print("c1 recv2:", c1.recv())
# c2 connected('recv') should be False (remote send side closed), send True
print("c2 connected recv/send:", c2.connected('recv'), c2.connected('send'))

# Closing one channel doesn't affect another
d1 = m1.open_channel(2, timeout=1); d2 = m2.accept_channel(timeout=1)
c1.close()
time.sleep(0.1)
try:
    c1.recv(timeout=0.1)
    print("ERROR c1 recv after close")
except EOFError:
    print("c1 recv EOF ok")
d1.send(b'still alive')
print("d2 got:", d2.recv())
print("d1 connected:", d1.connected(), "d2 connected:", d2.connected())

# Underlying death propagates EOF to all channels
l2 = listen(0); r2 = remote('localhost', l2.lport); srv2 = l2.wait_for_connection()
m3 = r2.mux(); m4 = srv2.mux()
e1 = m3.open_channel(1, timeout=1); e2 = m4.accept_channel(timeout=1)
r2.close()  # kill underlying from client side; but m3 reader may block...
# Instead use m3.close() which closes underlying and sends mux close to m4
m3.close()
time.sleep(0.2)
print("m4.closed after m3.close", m4.closed)
try:
    e2.recv(timeout=0.2)
    print("ERROR e2 recv expected EOF")
except EOFError:
    print("e2 recv EOF ok")
try:
    e2.send(b'x')
    print("ERROR e2 send expected EOF")
except EOFError:
    print("e2 send EOF ok")

m1.close(); m2.close(); m4.close()
print("HALFCLOSE OK")

import socket, time, threading
from pwnlib.tubes.listen import listen
from pwnlib.tubes.remote import remote
from pwnlib.tubes.mux import _MSG_MUX_CLOSE

l2 = listen(0); r2 = remote('localhost', l2.lport); srv2 = l2.wait_for_connection()
m3 = r2.mux(); m4 = srv2.mux()
e1 = m3.open_channel(1, timeout=1); e2 = m4.accept_channel(timeout=1)
print("m4 reader alive", m4._reader_thread.is_alive())
# monkeypatch dispatch on m4 to trace mux close
orig = m4._dispatch
def traced(cid, payload):
    if payload and payload[0] == _MSG_MUX_CLOSE:
        print("MUX CLOSE received on m4 channel", cid)
    return orig(cid, payload)
m4._dispatch = traced
print("closing m3")
m3.close()
time.sleep(0.5)

print("m4 reader alive after", m4._reader_thread.is_alive())
print("m3 reader alive after", m3._reader_thread.is_alive())

COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_concurrent.py <<'PYEOF'
import socket, time, threading, random
from pwnlib.tubes.listen import listen
from pwnlib.tubes.remote import remote

l = listen(0); r = remote('localhost', l.lport); srv = l.wait_for_connection()
m1 = r.mux(high_water_mark=1<<20, low_water_mark=1<<18)
m2 = srv.mux(high_water_mark=1<<20, low_water_mark=1<<18)

NCH = 8
NSEND = 50
SIZE = 1000

channels1 = [m1.open_channel(i+1, timeout=2) for i in range(NCH)]
channels2 = [m2.accept_channel(timeout=2) for _ in range(NCH)]
print("opened", len(channels2))

errors = []
lock = threading.Lock()

def sender(idx):
    try:
        ch = channels1[idx]
        for j in range(NSEND):
            payload = ("%d:%d:" % (idx, j)).encode().ljust(SIZE, b'x')
            ch.send(payload)
    except Exception as e:
        with lock:
            errors.append(("sender", idx, type(e).__name__, str(e)))

def receiver(idx):
    try:
        ch = channels2[idx]
        for j in range(NSEND):
            data = ch.recvn(SIZE, timeout=5)
            prefix = ("%d:%d:" % (idx, j)).encode()
            if not data.startswith(prefix):
                with lock:
                    errors.append(("badprefix", idx, j, data[:20]))
    except Exception as e:
        with lock:
            errors.append(("receiver", idx, type(e).__name__, str(e)))

threads = []
for i in range(NCH):
    t = threading.Thread(target=sender, args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
    t = threading.Thread(target=receiver, args=(i,)); t.start(); threads.append(t)
for t in threads:
    t.join(30)
print("alive", [t.is_alive() for t in threads])
print("errors", errors[:10])
if not errors:
    print("CONCURRENT OK")
# stats
for i in range(NCH):
    s1 = channels1[i].stats; s2 = channels2[i].stats
    assert s1['bytes_sent'] == s2['bytes_received'] == NSEND*SIZE, (i, s1, s2)
    assert s1['frames_sent'] == s2['frames_received'] == NSEND, (i, s1, s2)
print("stats all consistent")

m1.close(); m2.close()
PYEOF
timeout 60 python /tmp/test_concurrent.py
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
opened 8
alive [False, False, False, False, False, False, False, False, False, False, False, False, False, False, False, False]
errors []
CONCURRENT OK
stats all consistent

path = 'pwnlib/tubes/mux.py'
s = open(path).read()

old = '''    @property
    def high_water_mark(self):
        return self._high_water_mark

    @property
    def low_water_mark(self):
        return self._low_water_mark
'''
new = '''    @property
    def high_water_mark(self):
        return self._high_water_mark

    @high_water_mark.setter
    def high_water_mark(self, value):
        if value < self._low_water_mark:
            raise ValueError("high_water_mark cannot be below low_water_mark")
        self._high_water_mark = value

    @property
    def low_water_mark(self):
        return self._low_water_mark

    @low_water_mark.setter
    def low_water_mark(self, value):
        if value > self._high_water_mark:
            raise ValueError("low_water_mark cannot exceed high_water_mark")
        self._low_water_mark = value
'''
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new, 1)
open(path,'w').write(s)
print("setters added")

returncode=0

    def communicate(self, stdin = None):
        """communicate(stdin = None) -> str

        Calls :meth:`subprocess.Popen.communicate` method on the process.
        """

        return self.proc.communicate(stdin)

    # Implementation of the methods required for tube
    def recv_raw(self, numb):
        # This is a slight hack. We try to notice if the process is
        # dead, so we can write a message.
        self.poll()

        if not self.connected_raw('recv'):
            raise EOFError

        if not self.can_recv_raw(self.timeout):
            return ''

        if IS_WINDOWS:
            data = b''
            count = 0
            while count < numb:
                if self._read_queue.empty():
                    break
                last_byte = self._read_queue.get(block=False)
                data += last_byte
                count += 1
            return data

        # This will only be reached if we either have data,
        # or we have reached an EOF. In either case, it
        # should be safe to read without expecting it to block.
        data = ''

        try:
            data = self.proc.stdout.read(numb)
        except IOError:
            pass

        if not data:
            self.shutdown("recv")
            raise EOFError

        return data

    def send_raw(self, data):
        # This is a slight hack. We try to notice if the process is
        # dead, so we can write a message.
        self.poll()

        if not self.connected_raw('send'):
            raise EOFError

        try:
            self.proc.stdin.write(data)
            self.proc.stdin.flush()
        except IOError:
            raise EOFError

    def settimeout_raw(self, timeout):
        pass

    def can_recv_raw(self, timeout):
        if not self.connected_raw('recv'):
            return False

        if IS_WINDOWS:
            with self.countdown(timeout=timeout):
                while self.timeout and self._read_queue.empty() and self._read_thread.is_alive():
                    time.sleep(0.01)
                if not self._read_queue.empty():
                    return True
                if not self._read_thread.is_alive():
                    raise EOFError
                return False

        try:
            if timeout is None:
                return select.select([self.proc.stdout], [], []) == ([self.proc.stdout], [], [])

            return select.select([self.proc.stdout], [], [], timeout) == ([self.proc.stdout], [], [])
        except ValueError:
            # Not sure why this isn't caught when testing self.proc.stdout.closed,
            # but it's not.
            #
            #   File "/home/user/pwntools/pwnlib/tubes/process.py", line 112, in can_recv_raw
            #     return select.select([self.proc.stdout], [], [], timeout) == ([self.proc.stdout], [], [])
            # ValueError: I/O operation on closed file
            raise EOFError
        except select.error as v:
            if v.args[0] == errno.EINTR:
                return False

    def connected_raw(self, direction):
        if direction == 'any':
            return self.poll() is None
        elif direction == 'send':
            return self.proc.stdin and not self.proc.stdin.closed
        elif direction == 'recv':
            return self.proc.stdout and not self.proc.stdout.closed

    def close(self):
        if self.proc is None:
            return

        # First check if we are already dead
        self.poll()

        if not self._stop_noticed:
            try:
                self.proc.kill()
                self.proc.wait()
                self._stop_noticed = time.time()
                self.info('Stopped process %r (pid %i)' % (self.program, self.pid))
            except OSError:
                pass

        # close file descriptors
        for fd in [self.proc.stdin, self.proc.stdout, self.proc.stderr]:
            if fd is not None:
                try:
                    fd.close()
                except IOError as e:
                    if e.errno != errno.EPIPE and e.errno != errno.EINVAL:
                        raise


    def fileno(self):
        if not self.connected():
            self.error("A stopped process does not have a file number")

        return self.proc.stdout.fileno()

    def shutdown_raw(self, direction):
        if direction == "send":
            self.proc.stdin.close()

        if direction == "recv":
            self.proc.stdout.close()

        if all(fp is None or fp.closed for fp in [self.proc.stdin, self.proc.stdout]):
            self.close()

    def __pty_make_controlling_tty(self, tty_fd):
        '''This makes the pseudo-terminal the controlling tty. This should be
        more portable than the pty.fork() function. Specifically, this should
        work on Solaris. '''

        child_name = os.ttyname(tty_fd)

        # Disconnect from controlling tty. Harmless if not already connected.
        try:
            fd = os.open("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR | os.O_NOCTTY)
            if fd >= 0:

END STEP

COMMAND:
cd /app && python - <<'PYEOF'
path = 'pwnlib/tubes/mux.py'
s = open(path).read()

old = '''    def _reader_loop(self):
        try:
            while True:
                try:
                    header = self._underlying.recvn(_HDRLEN, timeout=Timeout.maximum)
                except EOFError:
                    break
                if not header or len(header) < _HDRLEN:
                    break

                channel_id, length = struct.unpack(_HDR_FMT, header)
                payload = self._underlying.recvn(length, timeout=Timeout.maximum)
                if payload is None or len(payload) < length:
                    break

                self._dispatch(channel_id, payload)
        except Exception:
            log.debug("TubeMultiplexer reader thread died", exc_info=True)
        finally:
            self._handle_underlying_eof()
'''
new = '''    def _reader_loop(self):
        try:
            while True:
                try:
                    header = self._underlying.recvn(_HDRLEN, timeout=_POLL_INTERVAL)
                except EOFError:
                    break
                if not header:
                    # recvn() timed out with no data.  Check whether the
                    # underlying tube has been closed out from under us, and
                    # if not keep polling.
                    if self._closed or self._underlying_dead():
                        break
                    continue
                if len(header) < _HDRLEN:
                    break

                channel_id, length = struct.unpack(_HDR_FMT, header)
                payload = self._underlying.recvn(length, timeout=Timeout.maximum)
                if payload is None or len(payload) < length:
                    break

                self._dispatch(channel_id, payload)
        except Exception:
            log.debug("TubeMultiplexer reader thread died", exc_info=True)
        finally:
            self._handle_underlying_eof()

    def _underlying_dead(self):
        """Best-effort check for whether the underlying tube has been closed."""
        underlying = self._underlying
        closed = getattr(underlying, "closed", None)
        if isinstance(closed, dict):
            return bool(closed.get("recv") or closed.get("send"))
        if closed is True:
            return True
        try:
            if hasattr(underlying, "proc"):
                return not underlying.connected()
        except Exception:
            pass
        return False
'''
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new, 1)

# Add poll interval constant near top
s = s.replace('''_MAX_CHANNEL_ID = 65535
''', '''_MAX_CHANNEL_ID = 65535

# How often the reader thread wakes up to check for a closed underlying tube.
_POLL_INTERVAL = 0.1
''', 1)

open(path,'w').write(s)
print("reader patched")

path = 'pwnlib/tubes/mux.py'

old = '''    def _handle_open(self, channel_id):
        if self._closed:
            return

        with self._channels_lock:
            if channel_id in self.channels:
                return
            if len(self.channels) >= self._max_channels:
                # Cannot accept another channel; reject the open request.
                try:
                    self._send_frame(channel_id, _MSG_NACK)
                except EOFError:
                    pass
                return
            channel = MuxChannel(self, channel_id)
            self.channels[channel_id] = channel

        try:
            self._send_frame(channel_id, _MSG_ACK)
        except EOFError:
            return

        with self._accept_cond:
            self._accept_queue.append(channel)
            self._accept_cond.notify_all()
'''
new = '''    def _handle_open(self, channel_id):
        if self._closed:
            return

        already_open = False
        with self._channels_lock:
            if channel_id in self.channels:
                # Both sides independently opened this channel.  Acknowledge
                # the peer's request using the channel we already have.
                already_open = True
            elif len(self.channels) >= self._max_channels:
                # Cannot accept another channel; reject the open request.
                try:
                    self._send_frame(channel_id, _MSG_NACK)
                except EOFError:
                    pass
                return
            else:
                channel = MuxChannel(self, channel_id)
                self.channels[channel_id] = channel

        if already_open:
            try:
                self._send_frame(channel_id, _MSG_ACK)
            except EOFError:
                pass
            return

        try:
            self._send_frame(channel_id, _MSG_ACK)
        except EOFError:
            return

        with self._accept_cond:
            self._accept_queue.append(channel)
            self._accept_cond.notify_all()
'''
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new, 1)

STEP 89 TOOL 1

cd /app && nl -ba pwnlib/tubes/mux.py | sed -n '1,220p'
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
     1	"""
     2	Multiplex multiple logical, bidirectional channels over a single underlying tube.
     3	
     4	This module provides :class:`TubeMultiplexer`, which frames data sent over a
     5	single underlying :class:`pwnlib.tubes.tube.tube` into multiple independent
     6	logical channels, and :class:`MuxChannel`, the tube-like object returned for
     7	each logical channel.
     8	"""
     9	
    10	import collections
    11	import logging
    12	import struct
    13	import threading
    14	import time
    15	
    16	from pwnlib.log import getLogger
    17	from pwnlib.timeout import Timeout
    18	from pwnlib.tubes.tube import tube
    19	
    20	log = getLogger(__name__)
    21	
    22	# Message types used in the framing protocol
    23	_MSG_OPEN   = 1   # Open a new channel
    24	_MSG_DATA   = 2   # Data for an open channel
    25	_MSG_CLOSE  = 3   # Fully close a channel
    26	_MSG_ACK    = 4   # Acknowledge a channel open
    27	_MSG_NACK   = 5   # Reject a channel open
    28	_MSG_PAUSE  = 6   # Ask the peer to pause sending on this channel
    29	_MSG_RESUME = 7   # Ask the peer to resume sending on this channel
    30	_MSG_FIN    = 8   # Half-close: local send direction is finished
    31	_MSG_MUX_CLOSE = 9  # The whole multiplexer is being closed
    32	
    33	_HDR_FMT = "!II"
    34	_HDRLEN = struct.calcsize(_HDR_FMT)
    35	
    36	_MAX_CHANNEL_ID = 65535
    37	
    38	# How often the reader thread wakes up to check for a closed underlying tube.
    39	_POLL_INTERVAL = 0.1
    40	
    41	
    42	class TubeMultiplexer(object):
    43	    """Multiplex multiple logical channels over one underlying tube.
    44	
    45	    Arguments:
    46	        underlying(tube): The underlying tube to multiplex over.
    47	        max_channels(int): Maximum number of simultaneously open channels.
    48	            Must be between 1 and 65535.
    49	        high_water_mark(int): Number of queued bytes for a channel which
    50	            causes the remote sender to be paused.
    51	        low_water_mark(int): Number of queued bytes for a channel which
    52	            causes the remote sender to be resumed.
    53	
    54	    Examples:
    55	
    56	        >>> import pwnlib.tubes.mux  # doctest: +SKIP
    57	    """
    58	
    59	    def __init__(self, underlying, max_channels=256,
    60	                 high_water_mark=1048576, low_water_mark=262144):
    61	        if not isinstance(underlying, tube):
    62	            raise TypeError("underlying must be an instance of pwnlib.tubes.tube.tube")
    63	        if not isinstance(max_channels, int) or isinstance(max_channels, bool) \
    64	                or max_channels < 1 or max_channels > _MAX_CHANNEL_ID:
    65	            raise ValueError("max_channels must be an integer in [1, 65535]")
    66	        if low_water_mark > high_water_mark:
    67	            raise ValueError("low_water_mark cannot exceed high_water_mark")
    68	
    69	        self._underlying = underlying
    70	        self._max_channels = max_channels
    71	        self._high_water_mark = high_water_mark
    72	        self._low_water_mark = low_water_mark
    73	
    74	        #: Dictionary mapping open channel IDs to their :class:`MuxChannel`.
    75	        self.channels = {}
    76	
    77	        self._channels_lock = threading.RLock()
    78	        self._write_lock = threading.Lock()
    79	        self._closed = False
    80	        self._closed_cond = threading.Condition()
    81	
    82	        # Queue of channels accepted from the remote side, for accept_channel()
    83	        self._accept_cond = threading.Condition()
    84	        self._accept_queue = collections.deque()
    85	
    86	        self._next_channel_id = 1
    87	
    88	        # Start the reader thread which consumes frames from the underlying tube.
    89	        self._reader_thread = threading.Thread(
    90	            target=self._reader_loop, daemon=True,
    91	            name="pwntools-tube-mux-reader")
    92	        self._reader_thread.start()
    93	
    94	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    95	    # Public properties
    96	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    97	    @property
    98	    def max_channels(self):
    99	        return self._max_channels
   100	
   101	    @property
   102	    def high_water_mark(self):
   103	        return self._high_water_mark
   104	
   105	    @high_water_mark.setter
   106	    def high_water_mark(self, value):
   107	        self._high_water_mark = value
   108	
   109	    @property
   110	    def low_water_mark(self):
   111	        return self._low_water_mark
   112	
   113	    @low_water_mark.setter
   114	    def low_water_mark(self, value):
   115	        self._low_water_mark = value
   116	
   117	    @property
   118	    def closed(self):
   119	        return self._closed
   120	
   121	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   122	    # Channel management
   123	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   124	    def open_channel(self, channel_id=None, timeout=None):
   125	        """Open a new logical channel and wait for remote acknowledgement.
   126	
   127	        Arguments:
   128	            channel_id(int): Explicit channel ID, or None to auto-allocate.
   129	            timeout(float): Maximum time in seconds to wait for the remote
   130	                acknowledgement.  ``None`` waits forever.
   131	
   132	        Returns:
   133	            The newly created :class:`MuxChannel`.
   134	
   135	        Raises:
   136	            TypeError: If ``channel_id`` is not an integer.
   137	            ValueError: If ``channel_id`` is out of range, already in use, or
   138	                exceeds the multiplexer capacity.
   139	            TimeoutError: If the remote does not acknowledge in time.
   140	            EOFError: If the multiplexer is closed.
   141	        """
   142	        if self._closed:
   143	            raise EOFError("multiplexer is closed")
   144	
   145	        if channel_id is None:
   146	            channel_id = self._allocate_channel_id()
   147	        else:
   148	            if not isinstance(channel_id, int) or isinstance(channel_id, bool):
   149	                raise TypeError("channel_id must be an integer")
   150	            if channel_id < 1 or channel_id > _MAX_CHANNEL_ID:
   151	                raise ValueError("channel_id must be in [1, 65535]")
   152	            if channel_id > self._max_channels:
   153	                raise ValueError("channel_id exceeds max_channels")
   154	            with self._channels_lock:
   155	                if channel_id in self.channels:
   156	                    raise ValueError("channel_id already in use")
   157	                if len(self.channels) >= self._max_channels:
   158	                    raise ValueError("max_channels exceeded")
   159	
   160	        channel = MuxChannel(self, channel_id)
   161	
   162	        with self._channels_lock:
   163	            if self._closed:
   164	                raise EOFError("multiplexer is closed")
   165	            if channel_id in self.channels:
   166	                raise ValueError("channel_id already in use")
   167	            if len(self.channels) >= self._max_channels:
   168	                raise ValueError("max_channels exceeded")
   169	            self.channels[channel_id] = channel
   170	
   171	        try:
   172	            self._send_frame(channel_id, _MSG_OPEN)
   173	        except EOFError:
   174	            self._remove_channel(channel_id)
   175	            raise
   176	
   177	        if not channel._open_event.wait(timeout):
   178	            # Clean up the failed channel and tell the remote side to forget it.
   179	            self._remove_channel(channel_id)
   180	            try:
   181	                self._send_frame(channel_id, _MSG_CLOSE)
   182	            except EOFError:
   183	                pass
   184	            raise TimeoutError("timed out waiting for channel acknowledgement")
   185	
   186	        if channel._open_failed:
   187	            self._remove_channel(channel_id)
   188	            raise EOFError("remote refused to open channel")
   189	
   190	        return channel
   191	
   192	    def _allocate_channel_id(self):
   193	        with self._channels_lock:
   194	            if len(self.channels) >= self._max_channels:
   195	                raise ValueError("max_channels exceeded")
   196	            start = self._next_channel_id
   197	            cid = start
   198	            for _ in range(self._max_channels):
   199	                if cid not in self.channels:
   200	                    self._next_channel_id = cid + 1
   201	                    if self._next_channel_id > self._max_channels:
   202	                        self._next_channel_id = 1
   203	                    return cid
   204	                cid += 1
   205	                if cid > self._max_channels:
   206	                    cid = 1
   207	                if cid == start:
   208	                    break
   209	            raise ValueError("max_channels exceeded")
   210	
   211	    def _remove_channel(self, channel_id):
   212	        with self._channels_lock:
   213	            self.channels.pop(channel_id, None)
   214	
   215	    def accept_channel(self, timeout=None):
   216	        """Wait for the remote side to open a channel.
   217	
   218	        Arguments:
   219	            timeout(float): Maximum time in seconds to wait.  ``None`` waits
   220	                forever.

END STEP

STEP 90 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && nl -ba pwnlib/tubes/mux.py | sed -n '220,500p'
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
[OUTPUT_HEAD]
   220	                forever.
   221	
   222	        Returns:
   223	            The accepted :class:`MuxChannel`, or ``None`` if the timeout
   224	            expired before a channel was opened.
   225	
   226	        Raises:
   227	            EOFError: If the multiplexer is closed.
   228	        """
   229	        deadline = None if timeout is None else time.time() + timeout
   230	
   231	        with self._accept_cond:
   232	            while not self._accept_queue and not self._closed:
   233	                if deadline is None:
   234	                    self._accept_cond.wait()
   235	                else:
   236	                    remaining = deadline - time.time()
   237	                    if remaining <= 0:
   238	                        return None
   239	                    self._accept_cond.wait(remaining)
   240	
   241	            if self._closed:
   242	                raise EOFError("multiplexer is closed")
   243	
   244	            if self._accept_queue:
   245	                return self._accept_queue.popleft()
   246	
   247	            return None
   248	
   249	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   250	    # Closing
   251	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   252	    def close(self):
   253	        """Close the multiplexer and all channels.
   254	
   255	        Signals EOF to every channel, closes the underlying tube, and is
   256	        idempotent.  Threads blocked in :meth:`accept_channel` are woken up
   257	        with :class:`EOFError`.
   258	        """
   259	        with self._closed_cond:
   260	            if self._closed:
   261	                return
   262	
   263	        # Tell the peer that the entire multiplexer is closing, so that it
   264	        # can detect the closure even if the underlying tube's EOF is delayed.
   265	        try:
   266	            self._send_frame(0, _MSG_MUX_CLOSE)
   267	        except Exception:
   268	            pass
   269	
   270	        with self._closed_cond:
   271	            if self._closed:
   272	                return
   273	            self._closed = True
   274	            self._closed_cond.notify_all()
   275	
   276	        with self._channels_lock:
   277	            channels = list(self.channels.values())
   278	            self.channels.clear()
   279	
   280	        for channel in channels:
   281	            channel._mux_closed()
   282	
   283	        with self._accept_cond:
   284	            self._accept_cond.notify_all()
   285	
   286	        # Wake our own reader thread and signal EOF to the peer.  A blocked
   287	        # recv() is woken by shutting down the socket, whereas merely calling
   288	        # close() may leave the reader thread stuck in recv().
   289	        try:
   290	            self._underlying.shutdown("send")
   291	        except Exception:
   292	            pass
   293	        try:
   294	            self._underlying.shutdown("recv")
   295	        except Exception:
   296	            pass
   297	        try:
   298	            self._underlying.close()
   299	        except Exception:
   300	            pass
   301	
   302	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   303	    # Reader thread
   304	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   305	    def _reader_loop(self):
   306	        try:
   307	            while True:
   308	                try:
   309	                    header = self._underlying.recvn(_HDRLEN, timeout=_POLL_INTERVAL)
   310	                except EOFError:
   311	                    break
   312	                if not header:
   313	                    # recvn() timed out with no data.  Check whether the
   314	                    # underlying tube has been closed out from under us, and
   315	                    # if not keep polling.
   316	                    if self._closed or self._underlying_dead():
   317	                        break
   318	                    continue
   319	                if len(header) < _HDRLEN:
   320	                    break
   321	
   322	                channel_id, length = struct.unpack(_HDR_FMT, header)
   323	                while True:
   324	                    payload = self._underlying.recvn(length, timeout=_POLL_INTERVAL)
   325	                    if payload and len(payload) >= length:
   326	                        break
   327	                    if self._closed or self._underlying_dead():
   328	                        raise EOFError("underlying tube closed while reading frame")
   329	
   330	                self._dispatch(channel_id, payload)
   331	        except Exception:
   332	            log.debug("TubeMultiplexer reader thread died", exc_info=True)
   333	        finally:
   334	            self._handle_underlying_eof()
   335

   380	    def _handle_open(self, channel_id):
   381	        if self._closed:
   382	            return
   383	
   384	        already_open = False
   385	        with self._channels_lock:
   386	            if channel_id in self.channels:
   387	                # Both sides independently opened this channel.  Acknowledge
   388	                # the peer's request using the channel we already have.
   389	                already_open = True
   390	            elif len(self.channels) >= self._max_channels:
   391	                # Cannot accept another channel; reject the open request.
   392	                try:
   393	                    self._send_frame(channel_id, _MSG_NACK)
   394	                except EOFError:
   395	                    pass
   396	                return
   397	            else:
   398	                channel = MuxChannel(self, channel_id)
   399	                self.channels[channel_id] = channel

   402	            try:
   403	                self._send_frame(channel_id, _MSG_ACK)
   404	            except EOFError:
   405	                pass
   406	            return
   407	
   408	        try:
   409	            self._send_frame(channel_id, _MSG_ACK)
   410	        except EOFError:
   411	            return
   412	
   413	        with self._accept_cond:
   414	            self._accept_queue.append(channel)
   415	            self._accept_cond.notify_all()
   416	
   417	    def _handle_ack(self, channel_id):
   418	        with self._channels_lock:
   419	            channel = self.channels.get(channel_id)
   420	        if channel is not None:
   421	            channel._open_event.set()
   422	
   423	    def _handle_nack(self, channel_id):
   424	        with self._channels_lock:
   425	            channel = self.channels.get(channel_id)
   426	        if channel is not None:
   427	            channel._open_failed = True
   428	            channel._open_event.set()
   429	
   430	    def _handle_data(self, channel_id, data):
   431	        with self._channels_lock:
   432	            channel = self.channels.get(channel_id)
   433	        if channel is not None:
   434	            channel._recv_data(data)
   435	
   436	    def _handle_close(self, channel_id):
   437	        with self._channels_lock:
   438	            channel = self.channels.pop(channel_id, None)
   439	        if channel is not None:
   440	            channel._remote_closed()
   441	
   442	    def _handle_fin(self, channel_id):
   443	        with self._channels_lock:
   444	            channel = self.channels.get(channel_id)
   445	        if channel is not None:
   446	            channel._remote_fin()
   447	
   448	    def _handle_pause(self, channel_id):
   449	        with self._channels_lock:
   450	            channel = self.channels.get(channel_id)
   451	        if channel is not None:
   452	            channel._remote_pause()
   453	
   454	    def _handle_resume(self, channel_id):
   455	        with self._channels_lock:
   456	            channel = self.channels.get(channel_id)
   457	        if channel is not None:
   458	            channel._remote_resume()
   459	
   460	    def _handle_underlying_eof(self):
   461	        """Propagate death of the underlying tube to all channels."""
   462	        with self._closed_cond:
   463	            if self._closed:
   464	                return
   465	            self._closed = True
   466	            self._closed_cond.notify_all()
   467	
   468	        with self._channels_lock:
   469	            channels = list(self.channels.values())
   470	            self.channels.clear()
   471	
   472	        for channel in channels:
   473	            channel._mux_closed()
   474	
   475	        with self._accept_cond:
   476	            self._accept_cond.notify_all()
   477	
   478	        try:
   479	            self._underlying.close()
   480	        except Exception:
   481	            pass
   482	
   483	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   484	    # Frame I/O
   485	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   486	    def _make_frame(self, channel_id, msgtype, body=b''):
   487	        payload = bytes((msgtype,)) + body
   488	        return struct.pack(_HDR_FMT, channel_id, len(payload)) + payload
   489	
   490	    def _send_frame(self, channel_id, msgtype, body=b''):
   491	        if self._closed:
   492	            raise EOFError("multiplexer is closed")
   493	
   494	        frame = self._make_frame(channel_id, msgtype, body)
   495	        try:
   496	            with self._write_lock:
   497	                self._underlying.send(frame)
   498	        except EOFError:

returncode=0
    def _underlying_dead(self):
        """Best-effort check for whether the underlying tube has been closed."""
        underlying = self._underlying
        closed = getattr(underlying, "closed", None)
        if isinstance(closed, dict):
            return bool(closed.get("recv") or closed.get("send"))
        if closed is True:
            return True
        try:
            if hasattr(underlying, "proc"):
                return not underlying.connected()
        except Exception:
            pass
        return False

    def _dispatch(self, channel_id, payload):
        if not payload:
            return

        msgtype = payload[0]
        body = payload[1:]

        if msgtype == _MSG_OPEN:
            self._handle_open(channel_id)
        elif msgtype == _MSG_ACK:
            self._handle_ack(channel_id)
        elif msgtype == _MSG_NACK:
            self._handle_nack(channel_id)
        elif msgtype == _MSG_DATA:
            self._handle_data(channel_id, body)
        elif msgtype == _MSG_CLOSE:
            self._handle_close(channel_id)
        elif msgtype == _MSG_FIN:
            self._handle_fin(channel_id)
        elif msgtype == _MSG_PAUSE:
            self._handle_pause(channel_id)
        elif msgtype == _MSG_RESUME:
            self._handle_resume(channel_id)
        elif msgtype == _MSG_MUX_CLOSE:
            self._handle_underlying_eof()
        else:
            log.debug("Unknown multiplexer message type %r on channel %r",
                      msgtype, channel_id)

    def _handle_open(self, channel_id):

            raise
        except Exception:
            self._handle_underlying_eof()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Flow control
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _maybe_pause(self, channel):
        send = False
        with channel._recv_cond:
            if not channel._flow_paused and \
                    channel._recv_size > self._high_water_mark:
                channel._flow_paused = True
                send = True
        if send:
            try:
                self._send_frame(channel.channel_id, _MSG_PAUSE)
            except EOFError:
                pass

    def _maybe_resume(self, channel):
        send = False
        with channel._recv_cond:
            if channel._flow_paused and \
                    channel._recv_size <= self._low_water_mark:
                channel._flow_paused = False
                send = True
        if send:
            try:
                self._send_frame(channel.channel_id, _MSG_RESUME)
            except EOFError:
                pass

    def _send_channel_data(self, channel, data):
        timeout = channel.timeout
        if timeout >= channel.maximum:
            deadline = None
        else:
            deadline = time.time() + timeout

        while True:
            with channel._send_cond:
                if self._closed or channel._send_eof:
                    raise EOFError("channel is closed")

                if not channel._peer_paused:
                    break

                if deadline is None:
                    channel._send_cond.wait()
                else:
                    remaining = deadline - time.time()
                    if remaining <= 0:
                        raise TimeoutError(
                            "timed out waiting for flow control on channel %r"
                            % channel.channel_id)
                    channel._send_cond.wait(remaining)

        self._send_frame(channel.channel_id, _MSG_DATA, data)

        with channel._stats_lock:
            channel._stats["bytes_sent"] += len(data)
            channel._stats["frames_sent"] += 1


class MuxChannel(tube):
    """A single logical channel inside a :class:`TubeMultiplexer`.

    Instances are tube objects, so they support the full ``recv``/``send``
    family of methods.
    """

    def __init__(self, mux, channel_id, timeout=tube.default):
        super(MuxChannel, self).__init__(timeout=timeout)
        self._mux = mux
        self._channel_id = channel_id

        # Receive path
        self._recv_queue = collections.deque()
        self._recv_size = 0
        self._recv_eof = False
        self._recv_cond = threading.Condition()

        # Send path / flow control
        self._send_eof = False
        self._send_cond = threading.Condition()
        self._peer_paused = False
        self._flow_paused = False

        # Open handshake
        self._open_event = threading.Event()
        self._open_failed = False

        # Statistics
        self._stats = {
            "bytes_sent": 0,
            "bytes_received": 0,
            "frames_sent": 0,
            "frames_received": 0,
        }
        self._stats_lock = threading.Lock()

        self.buffer.set_watermarks(high=mux.high_water_mark,
                                   low=mux.low_water_mark)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Public properties
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def channel_id(self):
        """The numeric ID of this channel."""
        return self._channel_id

    @property
    def stats(self):
        """A dict with byte/frame counters for this channel."""
        with self._stats_lock:
            return dict(self._stats)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

returncode=0
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Receive path
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _recv_data(self, data):
        """Called by the multiplexer reader thread when DATA arrives."""
        with self._recv_cond:
            self._recv_queue.append(data)
            self._recv_size += len(data)
            self._recv_cond.notify_all()

        with self._stats_lock:
            self._stats["bytes_received"] += len(data)
            self._stats["frames_received"] += 1

        self._mux._maybe_pause(self)

    def _recv_data_chunk(self, timeout=tube.default):
        """Block until a chunk is available or the timeout elapses."""
        timeout = self._get_timeout_seconds(timeout)
        deadline = None if timeout >= self.maximum else time.time() + timeout

        with self._recv_cond:
            while not self._recv_queue and not self._recv_eof:
                if deadline is None:
                    self._recv_cond.wait()
                else:
                    remaining = deadline - time.time()
                    if remaining <= 0:
                        return None
                    self._recv_cond.wait(remaining)

            if self._recv_eof and not self._recv_queue:
                raise EOFError("channel is closed")

            if not self._recv_queue:
                return None

            data = self._recv_queue.popleft()
            self._recv_size -= len(data)
            return data

    def _fillbuffer(self, timeout=tube.default):
        data = self._recv_data_chunk(timeout)
        if data:
            self.buffer.add(data)
            self._mux._maybe_resume(self)
        return data

    def _recv(self, numb=None, timeout=tube.default):
        numb = self.buffer.get_fill_size(numb)

        if not self.buffer and not self._fillbuffer(timeout):
            return b""

        result = self.buffer.get(numb)
        self._mux._maybe_resume(self)
        return result

    def recv_raw(self, numb):
        data = self._recv_data_chunk(self.timeout)
        if data is None:
            return None
        return data

    def can_recv_raw(self, timeout):
        with self._recv_cond:
            if self._recv_queue:
                return True
            if self._recv_eof:
                return False
            if timeout:
                self._recv_cond.wait(timeout)
            return bool(self._recv_queue)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Send path
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def send_raw(self, data):
        self._mux._send_channel_data(self, data)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Remote control messages
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _remote_pause(self):
        with self._send_cond:
            self._peer_paused = True

    def _remote_resume(self):
        with self._send_cond:
            self._peer_paused = False
            self._send_cond.notify_all()

    def _remote_fin(self):
        with self._recv_cond:
            self._recv_eof = True
            self._recv_cond.notify_all()

    def _remote_closed(self):
        with self._recv_cond:
            self._recv_eof = True
            self._recv_cond.notify_all()
        with self._send_cond:
            self._send_eof = True
            self._send_cond.notify_all()

    def _mux_closed(self):
        with self._recv_cond:
            self._recv_eof = True
            self._recv_cond.notify_all()
        with self._send_cond:
            self._send_eof = True
            self._send_cond.notify_all()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # tube interface
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def settimeout_raw(self, timeout):
        pass

    def connected_raw(self, direction):
        if self._mux.closed:
            return False

        if direction == "recv":
            return not self._recv_eof
        if direction == "send":
            return not self._send_eof
        if direction == "any":
            return not (self._recv_eof and self._send_eof)
        return False

    def shutdown_raw(self, direction):
        if direction == "send":
            with self._send_cond:
                if self._send_eof:
                    return
                self._send_eof = True
                self._send_cond.notify_all()
            try:
                self._mux._send_frame(self._channel_id, _MSG_FIN)
            except EOFError:
                pass

        elif direction == "recv":
            with self._recv_cond:
                if self._recv_eof:
                    return
                self._recv_eof = True
                self._recv_cond.notify_all()

    def close(self):
        """Close this channel and signal EOF to the remote peer."""
        with self._recv_cond:
            recv_already = self._recv_eof
            self._recv_eof = True
            self._recv_cond.notify_all()
        with self._send_cond:
            send_already = self._send_eof
            self._send_eof = True
            self._send_cond.notify_all()


END STEP

import sys
from pwnlib.tubes.tube import tube

class StdioTube(tube):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(timeout=None)
    def recv_raw(self, numb):
        data = sys.stdin.buffer.read(numb)
        if not data:
            raise EOFError
        return data
    def send_raw(self, data):
        sys.stdout.buffer.write(data)
        sys.stdout.buffer.flush()
    def can_recv_raw(self, timeout):
        import select
        r, _, _ = select.select([sys.stdin], [], [], timeout)
        return bool(r)
    def connected_raw(self, direction):
        return True

t = StdioTube()
m = t.mux(max_channels=8)
c = m.accept_channel(timeout=5)
data = c.recv(timeout=2)
c.send(b'echo:' + data)
c.close()
m.close()

class StdioTube(tube):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(timeout=None)
    def recv_raw(self, numb):
        r, _, _ = select.select([sys.stdin], [], [], self.timeout)
        if not r:
            return None
        data = os.read(sys.stdin.fileno(), numb)
        if not data:
            raise EOFError
        return data
    def send_raw(self, data):
        sys.stdout.buffer.write(data)
        sys.stdout.buffer.flush()
    def can_recv_raw(self, timeout):
        r, _, _ = select.select([sys.stdin], [], [], timeout)
        return bool(r)
    def connected_raw(self, direction):
        return True

dbg('child starting')
t = StdioTube()
m = t.mux(max_channels=8)
dbg('mux created')
c = m.accept_channel(timeout=5)
dbg('accepted', c.channel_id if c else None)
if c is None:
    dbg('no channel accepted')
    sys.exit(2)
data = c.recv(timeout=2)
dbg('got data', data)
c.send(b'echo:' + data)
c.close()
m.close()
dbg('done')
PYEOF

path = 'pwnlib/tubes/mux.py'
s = open(path).read()

# In open_channel, after wait, check closed
old = '''        if not channel._open_event.wait(timeout):
            # Clean up the failed channel and tell the remote side to forget it.
            self._remove_channel(channel_id)
            try:
                self._send_frame(channel_id, _MSG_CLOSE)
            except EOFError:
                pass
            raise TimeoutError("timed out waiting for channel acknowledgement")

        if channel._open_failed:
'''
new = '''        if not channel._open_event.wait(timeout):
            # Clean up the failed channel and tell the remote side to forget it.
            self._remove_channel(channel_id)
            try:
                self._send_frame(channel_id, _MSG_CLOSE)
            except EOFError:
                pass
            raise TimeoutError("timed out waiting for channel acknowledgement")

        if self._closed:
            self._remove_channel(channel_id)
            raise EOFError("multiplexer is closed")

        if channel._open_failed:
'''
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new, 1)

# _mux_closed sets open_event
old = '''    def _mux_closed(self):
        with self._recv_cond:
            self._recv_eof = True
            self._recv_cond.notify_all()
        with self._send_cond:
            self._send_eof = True
            self._send_cond.notify_all()
'''
new = '''    def _mux_closed(self):
        with self._recv_cond:
            self._recv_eof = True
            self._recv_cond.notify_all()
        with self._send_cond:
            self._send_eof = True
            self._send_cond.notify_all()
        self._open_event.set()
'''
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new, 1)

open(path,'w').write(s)
print("patched open-chunk-on-close")

import time, threading
from pwnlib.tubes.listen import listen
from pwnlib.tubes.remote import remote
l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost',l.lport); s=l.wait_for_connection()
m1=r.mux(); m2=s.mux()
res={}
def opener():
    try:
        m1.open_channel(timeout=None)
        res['r']='opened'
    except EOFError:
        res['r']='EOFError'
    except Exception as e:
        res['r']=type(e).__name__
t=threading.Thread(target=opener); t.start()
time.sleep(0.2)
print("before close alive", t.is_alive())

t.join(2)

"""
Multiplex multiple logical, bidirectional channels over a single underlying tube.

This module provides :class:`TubeMultiplexer`, which frames data sent over a
single underlying :class:`pwnlib.tubes.tube.tube` into multiple independent
logical channels, and :class:`MuxChannel`, the tube-like object returned for
each logical channel.
"""

import collections
import struct
import threading
import time

from pwnlib.log import getLogger
from pwnlib.timeout import Timeout
from pwnlib.tubes.tube import tube

log = getLogger(__name__)

import time
from pwnlib.tubes.listen import listen
from pwnlib.tubes.remote import remote
fail=0
for i in range(20):
    l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost',l.lport); s=l.wait_for_connection()
    m1=r.mux(); m2=s.mux()
    t0=time.time()
    try:
        c1=m1.open_channel(i+1, timeout=0.05)
        c2=m2.accept_channel(timeout=0.05)
        dt=time.time()-t0
        if dt>0.05:
            fail+=1
            print("slow", i, dt)
    except Exception as e:
        fail+=1
        print("fail", i, type(e).__name__)
    m1.close(); m2.close()
    r.close(); l.close()
print("failures", fail)
EOF

# Changelog

This changelog only includes added major features and changes. Bugfixes and
minor changes are omitted.

## Release History

The table below shows which release corresponds to each branch, and what date the version was released.

## 5.0.0 (`dev`)

- [#2677][2677] refactor: replace unsafe eval with safeeval.const in ROP cache loading
- [#2675][2675] feat(term): add zellij support
- [#2652][2652] Make setting the context.terminal to kitty more user friendly
- [#2638][2638] feat: add disable_corefiles context option
- [#2627][2627] remove pwnlib.util.iters.lookahead (broken anyway)
- [#2598][2598] aarch64: Fix ABI definition
- [#2419][2419] riscv: avoid compressed instructions (if you need compressed, use .option rvc)
- [#2551][2551] Detect when kitty is being used as terminal
- [#2519][2519] Drop Python 2.7 support / Require Python 3.10
- [#2507][2507] Add `+LINUX` and `+WINDOWS` doctest options and start proper testing on Windows
- [#2522][2522] Support starting a kitty debugging window with the 'kitten' command
- [#2524][2524] Raise EOFError during `process.recv` when stdout closes on Windows
- [#2526][2526] Properly make use of extra arguments in `packing` utilities. `sign` parameter requires keyword syntax to specify it.
- [#2517][2517] Allow to passthru kwargs on `ssh.__getattr__` convenience function to fix SSH motd problems
- [#2530][2530] Do NOT error when passing directory arguments in `checksec` commandline tool.
- [#2529][2529] Add LoongArch64 support
- [#2506][2506] ROP: fix `ROP(ELF(exe)).leave` is `None` in some ELF
- [#2504][2504] doc: add example case for `tuple` (host, port pair) in `gdb.attach`
- [#2546][2546] ssh: Allow passing disabled_algorithms keyword argument from ssh to paramiko
- [#2538][2538] Add `ssh -L` / `ssh.connect_remote()` workaround when `AllowTcpForwarding` is disabled
- [#2574][2574] Allow creating an ELF from in-memory bytes
- [#2575][2575] Detect when Terminator is being used as terminal
- [#2578][2578] Add gnome-terminal, Alacritty, Ttilix for run_in_new_terminal
- [#2590][2590] Add support for finding corefiles under WSL2
- [#2496][2496] Add linux ko file search support
- [#2542][2542] Decode `_IO_*` flags in `FileStructure` member
- [#2592][2592] pwnlib.config: Fix customization of `context.timeout`
- [#2608][2608] Abort on `libcdb file libc.so --unstrip` if eu-unstrip is not installed
- [#2611][2611] Cleanup `pwnlib.lexer` exports and imports
- [#2610][2610] Fix `log.progress` ignoring `context.log_console`
- [#2615][2615] tube/process: Fix redirecting stderr to stdout on Windows
- [#2639][2639] ROP: Remove stdout and argv workaround in ROPgadget invocation
- [#2630][2630] support `preexec_fn` in `debug()`
- [#2641][2641] support preexec_args in process
- [#2646][2646] fix(libcdb-cli): return early if no matched libc found
- [#2629][2629] Add `terminate()` method to process class that sends SIGTERM
- [#2643][2643] Refactor getdents.py, add support for SYS_getdents64
- [#2647][2647] packing: Add `overlap` to overlap structures easily
- [#2669][2669] asm: try native binutils before fallback architectures
- [#2673][2673] Add libc module for libc-related functions
- [#2680][2680] Cleanup Python 2 legacy
- [#2687][2687] Add (un)pack shorthands for 40-56 bit numbers `u48()`/`p48()`

+    def set_watermarks(self, high=None, low=None):
+        """
+        Set the high and/or low water marks used for flow control.
+
+        Arguments:
+            high(int): High water mark.  The buffer is considered
+                :attr:`over_high_water` when its size is greater than or
+                equal to this value.
+            low(int): Low water mark.  The buffer is considered
+                :attr:`under_low_water` when its size is less than or
+                equal to this value.
+
+        Raises:
+            ValueError: If both water marks are set and ``low > high``.
+        """
+        new_high = self.high_water if high is None else high
+        new_low = self.low_water if low is None else low
+        if new_high is not None and new_low is not None and new_low > new_high:
+            raise ValueError("low_water_mark cannot exceed high_water_mark")
+        if high is not None:
+            self.high_water = high
+        if low is not None:
+            self.low_water = low
+
+    @property
+    def over_high_water(self):
+        """True if the buffer size is at or above the high water mark."""
+        if self.high_water is None:
+            return False
+        return self.size >= self.high_water
+
+    @property
+    def under_low_water(self):
+        """True if the buffer size is at or below the low water mark."""
+        if self.low_water is None:
+            return False
+        return self.size <= self.low_water
+
     def index(self, x):
         """
         >>> b = Buffer()

# Add helper in TubeMultiplexer after __init__ or before open_channel
helper = '''
    def _resolve_timeout(self, timeout):
        """Resolve a timeout argument, honouring Timeout.default."""
        if timeout is Timeout.default:
            return self._underlying.timeout
        return timeout

'''
marker = '''    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Public properties
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
'''

'''

'''

'''

STEP 133 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > /tmp/mux_peer_exit.py <<'PYEOF'
import sys, os, select
from pwnlib.tubes.tube import tube
class StdioTube(tube):
    def recv_raw(self, numb):
        r,_,_ = select.select([sys.stdin], [], [], self.timeout)
        if not r: return None
        data = os.read(sys.stdin.fileno(), numb)
        if not data: raise EOFError
        return data
    def send_raw(self, data): sys.stdout.buffer.write(data); sys.stdout.buffer.flush()
    def can_recv_raw(self, timeout):
        r,_,_ = select.select([sys.stdin], [], [], timeout); return bool(r)
    def connected_raw(self, direction): return True
t = StdioTube()
m = t.mux()
c = m.accept_channel(timeout=5)
print("peer accepted", flush=True)
sys.stdout.buffer.flush()
# just exit without closing
EOF
timeout 20 python - <<'EOF'
import time
from pwnlib.tubes.process import process
p = process(['python3', '/tmp/mux_peer_exit.py'])
m = p.mux()
c = m.open_channel(1, timeout=2)
print("opened")
time.sleep(0.3)  # child exits after accepting
time.sleep(0.3)
print("m.closed", m.closed)
try:
    c.recv(timeout=0.2)
    print("ERROR no EOF")
except EOFError:
    print("channel EOF on underlying death ok")
m.close()
EOF

    from pwnlib.tubes.tube import tube

            r,_,_ = select.select([sys.stdin], [], [], self.timeout)

from pwnlib.tubes.listen import listen
from pwnlib.tubes.remote import remote
l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost',l.lport); s=l.wait_for_connection()
m1=r.mux(max_channels=100); m2=s.mux(max_channels=100)

    c=m1.open_channel(timeout=1)
    ch1.append(c)
    ch2.append(m2.accept_channel(timeout=1))
print("ids", [c.channel_id for c in ch1])
print("channels dict len", len(m1.channels), len(m2.channels))
# send on a few
for i in range(30):
    ch1[i].send(bytes([i])*10)
for i in range(30):
    assert ch2[i].recv(timeout=1) == bytes([i])*10, i
print("30 channels ok")
m1.close(); m2.close()
EOF

"""
Multiplex multiple logical, bidirectional channels over a single underlying tube.

This module provides :class:`TubeMultiplexer`, which frames data sent over a
single underlying :class:`pwnlib.tubes.tube.tube` into multiple independent
logical channels, and :class:`MuxChannel`, the tube-like object returned for
each logical channel.
"""

import collections
import struct
import threading
import time

from pwnlib.log import getLogger
from pwnlib.timeout import Timeout
from pwnlib.tubes.tube import tube

log = getLogger(__name__)

# Message types used in the framing protocol
_MSG_OPEN   = 1   # Open a new channel
_MSG_DATA   = 2   # Data for an open channel
_MSG_CLOSE  = 3   # Fully close a channel
_MSG_ACK    = 4   # Acknowledge a channel open
_MSG_NACK   = 5   # Reject a channel open
_MSG_PAUSE  = 6   # Ask the peer to pause sending on this channel
_MSG_RESUME = 7   # Ask the peer to resume sending on this channel
_MSG_FIN    = 8   # Half-close: local send direction is finished
_MSG_MUX_CLOSE = 9  # The whole multiplexer is being closed

_HDR_FMT = "!II"
_HDRLEN = struct.calcsize(_HDR_FMT)

_MAX_CHANNEL_ID = 65535

__all__ = ["TubeMultiplexer", "MuxChannel"]

# How often the reader thread wakes up to check for a closed underlying tube.
_POLL_INTERVAL = 0.01


class TubeMultiplexer(object):
    """Multiplex multiple logical channels over one underlying tube.


END STEP

import sys, os, select
from pwnlib.tubes.tube import tube
class StdioTube(tube):
    def recv_raw(self, numb):
        r,_,_ = select.select([sys.stdin], [], [], self.timeout)
        if not r: return None
        data = os.read(sys.stdin.fileno(), numb)
        if not data: raise EOFError
        return data
    def send_raw(self, data): sys.stdout.buffer.write(data); sys.stdout.buffer.flush()
    def can_recv_raw(self, timeout):
        r,_,_ = select.select([sys.stdin], [], [], timeout); return bool(r)
    def connected_raw(self, direction): return True
t = StdioTube()
m = t.mux()
c = m.accept_channel(timeout=5)
# die abruptly, closing stdout so parent sees EOF
os._exit(0)
EOF

    def _resolve_timeout(self, timeout):
        """Resolve a timeout argument, honouring Timeout.default."""
        if timeout is Timeout.default:
            return self._underlying.timeout
        return timeout

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Public properties
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def max_channels(self):
        return self._max_channels

    @property
    def high_water_mark(self):
        return self._high_water_mark

    @high_water_mark.setter
    def high_water_mark(self, value):
        self._high_water_mark = value

    @property
    def low_water_mark(self):
        return self._low_water_mark

    @low_water_mark.setter
    def low_water_mark(self, value):
        self._low_water_mark = value

    @property
    def closed(self):
        return self._closed

import threading
from pwnlib.tubes.listen import listen
from pwnlib.tubes.remote import remote
l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost',l.lport); s=l.wait_for_connection()
m1=r.mux(); m2=s.mux()
results=[]
def open_some(base):
    for i in range(5):
        c=m1.open_channel(base+i, timeout=1)
        results.append(c.channel_id)
ts=[threading.Thread(target=open_some, args=(i*10,)) for i in range(3)]
for t in ts: t.start()
for t in ts: t.join()
accepted=[m2.accept_channel(timeout=1).channel_id for _ in range(15)]
print("opened", sorted(results))
print("accepted", sorted(accepted))
assert sorted(results)==sorted(accepted)
print("concurrent open/accept ok")
m1.close(); m2.close()
EOF

from pwnlib.tubes.remote import remote

from pwnlib.tubes.remote import remote

    for _ in range(10):
        c=m1.open_channel(timeout=1)

        send = False

            if not channel._flow_paused and \\
                    channel._recv_size > self._high_water_mark:
                channel._flow_paused = True
                send = True

        send = False
        with channel._recv_cond:
            total = channel._recv_size + len(channel.buffer)
            if not channel._flow_paused and \\
                    total > self._high_water_mark:

                send = True

path = 'pwnlib/tubes/mux.py'
s = open(path).read()

old = '''    def _maybe_pause(self, channel):
        send = False
        with channel._recv_cond:
            total = channel._recv_size + len(channel.buffer)
            if not channel._flow_paused and \\
                    total > self._high_water_mark:
                channel._flow_paused = True
                send = True
'''
new = '''    def _maybe_pause(self, channel):
        send = False
        high = channel.buffer.high_water
        if high is None:
            high = self._high_water_mark
        with channel._recv_cond:
            total = channel._recv_size + len(channel.buffer)
            if not channel._flow_paused and \\
                    total > high:
                channel._flow_paused = True
                send = True
'''
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new, 1)

old = '''    def _maybe_resume(self, channel):
        send = False
        with channel._recv_cond:
            total = channel._recv_size + len(channel.buffer)
            if channel._flow_paused and \\
                    total <= self._low_water_mark:
                channel._flow_paused = False
                send = True
'''
new = '''    def _maybe_resume(self, channel):
        send = False
        low = channel.buffer.low_water
        if low is None:
            low = self._low_water_mark
        with channel._recv_cond:
            total = channel._recv_size + len(channel.buffer)
            if channel._flow_paused and \\
                    total <= low:
                channel._flow_paused = False
                send = True
'''
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new, 1)

open(path,'w').write(s)
print("flow control respects channel buffer watermarks")

import threading
from pwnlib.tubes.listen import listen
from pwnlib.tubes.remote import remote
l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost',l.lport); s=l.wait_for_connection()
m1=r.mux(); m2=s.mux()
c1=m1.open_channel(1, timeout=1); c2=m2.accept_channel(timeout=1)
N=20; SIZE=100
def sender(idx):
    for i in range(N):
        c1.send(("%d:%d:"%(idx,i)).encode().ljust(SIZE,b'x'))
ts=[threading.Thread(target=sender, args=(i,)) for i in range(4)]
for t in ts: t.start()
for t in ts: t.join()
received=[]
while len(received) < N*4:
    received.append(c2.recvn(SIZE, timeout=2))
print("received", len(received))
# verify all frames unique by prefix
prefixes = set(r[:5] for r in received)
print("unique", len(prefixes))
assert len(prefixes)==N*4
print("same-channel concurrent send ok")
m1.close(); m2.close()
EOF
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
received 80
unique 80
same-channel concurrent send ok

END STEP

import time, threading
from pwnlib.tubes.listen import listen
from pwnlib.tubes.remote import remote
l=listen(0); r=remote('localhost',l.lport); s=l.wait_for_connection()
m1=r.mux(high_water_mark=10, low_water_mark=1); m2=s.mux(high_water_mark=10, low_water_mark=1)
c1=m1.open_channel(1, timeout=1); c2=m2.accept_channel(timeout=1)
c1.send(b'A'*100)
time.sleep(0.2)
res={}
def sender():
    try:
        c1.send(b'B')
        res['r']='sent'
    except EOFError:
        res['r']='EOFError'
t=threading.Thread(target=sender); t.start()
time.sleep(0.2)
c1.shutdown('send')
t.join(2)
print("shutdown send unblocks sender:", res)
m1.close(); m2.close()
EOF

        with self._recv_cond:
            if self._recv_queue:
                return True
            if self._recv_eof:
                return False
            if timeout:
                self._recv_cond.wait(timeout)

        with self._recv_cond:
            if self._recv_queue:
                return True
            if self._recv_eof:
                return False
            if timeout is None:
                while not self._recv_queue and not self._recv_eof:
                    self._recv_cond.wait()
            elif timeout:
                self._recv_cond.wait(timeout)
            return bool(self._recv_queue)
'''

    m1.open_channel(i+1, timeout=1)

    with lock: got.append(c.channel_id if c else None)
